## 56. A label for every conversation without a job: Postie makes them itself and links the trail (owner's yes 5 Nov 2026, built the same day in `feat/postie-labels`, PR into `staging`)

Owner, 5 Nov 2026: nothing is getting labels, only the GCW dispute one exists. Any email trail without a job where we have conversed more than once should get a label, and every conversation on that matter linked under it.

Why nothing gets a label today (production, 5 Nov): "Let Postie make labels itself" (Settings > Email Agent) is off on all four mailboxes, so Postie only suggests; 26 suggestions sit on single threads waiting for an Add tap in the thread view that nobody opens, with near-duplicates among them ("Supplier dispute: DPA Energy", "DPA Energy account dispute", "Supplier dispute: DPA Solar"; three NETCC names; two workers comp names). The rule is also narrow on purpose: only a named dispute, claim, application or council matter, and only threads the sort flagged. In the last 60 days 154 conversations with no job have mail both ways and 2 or more messages; 2 labels hold 18 threads.

1. **The rule turns around.** A conversation with no job tagged, with mail both ways and 2 or more messages, is a matter and gets a label (supplier account, warranty, accreditation, training, an enquiry that went back and forth, a dispute). One-off emails, Subscriptions and updates, job-tagged threads and a staff member's personal matter on the shared inbox do not. The label pass runs on every such thread, not only the ones the sort flagged.
2. **Postie makes the label itself** when it is sure (the other party's name is in the thread), in one shape, "Who: what" ("DPA Energy: account dispute", "NETCC: Approved Seller application"). When unsure it still suggests. The Settings switch stays as the off switch but goes on for all four mailboxes (production data change, owner's yes).
3. **One label per matter.** Before making a label Postie sees the existing labels and the open suggestions; the same other party and the same subject means the same label, never a second name for it. The 26 suggestions waiting today are grouped into labels in the same build and their threads filed.
4. **The trail links itself.** A new label runs the existing 60 day sweep (`sweepLabels`), so every earlier thread on that matter is filed under it; later mail from the same party is filed by the label pass as now. Labels show in the Inbox side list and in Gmail as today.

Done means: on staging, two threads from the same fake supplier with mail both ways and no job get one label within an hour of the next Postie run, both filed under it; a one-off email gets none; a third thread from that supplier is filed under the same label, not a new one; on production after the yes, the 26 suggestions become labels (about 15 after grouping) with their threads filed, listed in the Inbox side list.


**As built (5 Nov 2026):** the owner turned "Let Postie make labels itself" on for sales@ and jasper@ (guy@ and nathan@ stay off, their own choice). The older-conversations pass and the one-off grouping live in `_shared/email-matters.js`, called from Postie's run before the label sweeps. A suggestion the grouping leaves unfiled goes back to the older pass, so the one-off runs once (it stops by itself after 30 Nov 2026; delete it then). A label Postie makes sweeps only mail from the same senders, so 50 new labels do not mean 3,000 model calls. Its sweeps never file a job thread. Our own reply on a one-off (`_shared/gmail-sync.js`) hands it back to the older pass, since it is now a conversation. Known: a staff member's claim the model turned down is read again on each new reply (one label call each), and a person who removes an AI label from a conversation may see Postie add it back on the supplier's next reply (there is no record of removed labels yet). Dismissing a suggestion now stores '' so the older pass leaves it alone (`email-actions.js`). Three conversation cases added to `agents/evals/postie/cases.json`; **the 9 model cases must pass on the laptop (`ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=... node agents/evals/postie/run.js`) before release**, this cloud session has no key.
Touches: `netlify/functions/_shared/email-agent.js` (candidate rule, label pass, dedupe against suggestions), `agents/prompts/postie-labels.js` (the prompt: matters, not only disputes; the name shape), `netlify/functions/mailbox-work-background.js` (one-off grouping of the waiting suggestions), `public/settings.html` wording only, `docs/PROJECT_SPEC.md` A18, `docs/AI_AGENTS_PLAN.md`. Overlaps: item 55 (waiting on yes) touches `email-agent.js`; build 55 and 56 in one chat, one after the other. Item 37 step 5 (Postie filing, built) is what this changes. Kind: feature (into `staging`); migration no (`mailboxes.auto_labels` and `email_labels` exist); money no, stock no, RLS no, payroll no; one production data change (`auto_labels` on for 4 mailboxes, and the labels made from the 26 suggestions). Size: medium (a morning). Test on staging: sales@staging.test, Inbox side list, Settings > Email Agent; the AI key and the Gmail key on staging. Best model: Opus 5.5, effort high, because it is an agent rule and prompt change with a known cause, no migration.

## 58. Pylon is design only: the pull fills the quote, the layout goes on the proposal, battery STCs worked out from usable kWh (planned 6 Nov 2026, waiting on the owner's yes)

Owner, 6 Nov 2026, after the first real pull (Q for 5 Matilda Road, Gaven, design `Voh4FslpnQ`): "It pulled it from Pylon but didn't put the STC amounts or the inverter or panels into the quote." Then: the ROI is worked out in our app (the savings page already does it); Pylon is only for the design; the only parts of the Pylon link anyone looks at are the layout, the warranty documents and the data sheets; pricing and terms live in our app; and if Pylon cannot give the battery STC count, work it out from the usable kWh on the design.

Today the pull only saves `projects.pylon_data` and shows a summary on the proposal cover (spec A16, display only). System size and Battery usable kWh take Pylon's figures only into a blank field as the page loads; the equipment never becomes line items; the BSTC count is typed by hand. What Pylon's API gives per design: `summary.dc_output_kw`, `summary.storage_kwh` (usable), `summary.latest_snapshot_url` (the layout image), the web and PDF proposal links, and per panel, inverter and battery type a `sku`, `quantity`, `description` and `type_url`. No STC or BSTC counts.

1. **One pull fills the quote.** Pull from Pylon fills System size (kW), the battery's nominal kWh (as a hint beside Usable kWh, see point 3) and the proposal link straight away; a field that already holds a different typed figure shows "Pylon says 33.95, use it" next to it, one tap. The pull also adds one material line per Pylon item (panels, inverters, batteries, Pylon's quantity) to the first stage at $0 with the Needs a price tag; staff swap each for the Stock item with the existing Search material picker. Pulling again replaces the Pylon lines it added, never typed or priced ones. No Stock matching and no saved SKU (a later add-on if retyping annoys).
2. **Layout on the proposal.** The "Your system" page shows Pylon's layout image (`latest_snapshot_url`, copied into the quote's documents so it does not depend on Pylon's link staying up) instead of the "Pylon design and ROI" link. Data sheets and warranty documents: checked during the build whether Pylon's product endpoints (`type_url`) carry them; if so, they are filed in the quote's Documents on the pull, else staff attach them as now.
3. **Battery STCs worked out, from a usable figure we own.** Pylon's `storage_kwh` is the nominal size (ten SigenStor BAT 10.0 read 90.4, usable is 8.76 each, 87.6), so the usable kWh comes from a TEops list of battery models (new table `battery_models`: Pylon SKU, description, nominal kWh per unit, usable kWh per unit, entered by and when; Settings > Battery models card to see and correct it). The first pull that meets a battery not on the list asks once, "Usable kWh per SigenStor BAT 10.0?", from the data sheet or the CEC list; every later quote works it out by itself, quantity x usable per unit. If Pylon's battery product record (`type_url`) carries a usable figure it is offered as the suggestion, never used on its own. The BSTC block shows the working, "87.6 kWh usable = 10 x 8.76, SigenStor BAT 10.0, entered 6 Nov 2026", an install date, and the count: usable kWh capped at 50, split into the program's tiers, each tier times its share of the period's factor, rounded down. A typed count or a typed usable figure wins and the page says it differs from the worked one. The factors and tiers are data in Settings (new "Battery STC factors" card on the Terms tab, one row per period: from date, factor per usable kWh, tier 1 to 14 kWh %, tier 14 to 28 kWh %, tier 28 to 50 kWh %), seeded by the migration with the DCCEEW schedule (confirmed 6 Nov 2026 from the government's Cheaper Home Batteries page): 9.3 flat to 31 Dec 2025; 8.4 flat 1 Jan to 30 Apr 2026; then tiered at 100% (0 to 14 kWh), 60% (over 14 to 28 kWh) and 15% (over 28 to 50 kWh) with the factor 6.8 from 1 May 2026, 5.7 from 1 Jan 2027, 5.2 from 1 Jul 2027, 4.6 from 1 Jan 2028, 4.1 from 1 Jul 2028, 3.6 from 1 Jan 2029, 3.1 from 1 Jul 2029, 2.6 from 1 Jan 2030, 2.1 from 1 Jul 2030. Worked example for the Matilda Road design (87.6 kWh usable, installed 2026 after 1 May): 50 kWh counted, 14 x 6.8 + 14 x 6.8 x 0.6 + 22 x 6.8 x 0.15 = 95.2 + 57.12 + 22.44 = 174.76, rounded down to 174 STCs. The proposal and the ROI keep using the BSTC credit as now (count x $ per STC, capped on the server).
4. **Field renamed** "Pylon design ID", hint: "the last part of the Pylon address bar, after /map/edit/<project>/".

Migration (one): `battery_models` (fast RLS, read by authenticated, written by pricing roles, index on `pylon_sku`), `company_settings.bstc_factors jsonb` seeded as above. `projects.battery_kwh` keeps the usable figure. No money function changes (the credit cap stays as it is), no stock, RLS beyond the new table, or payroll change.

Done means: on staging (admin@staging.test, the real Pylon key entered once in Settings > API Keys on the staging site) (1) open a Solar quote, paste `Voh4FslpnQ`, Pull from Pylon: System size 33.95, the hint "Pylon: 90.4 kWh nominal" beside Usable kWh, proposal link filled, three $0 lines on the first stage with quantities 70, 2 and 10, each tagged Needs a price; (2) the BSTC block shows the count worked out from 50 kWh (capped) at today's factor and tiers, and the $ total; type a count and it wins; (3) Settings > Terms > Battery STC factors shows the four periods and a changed share changes the count on the quote; (4) the proposal's Your system page shows the Pylon layout image and no Pylon link; (5) pull again after pricing the panel line: the panel line keeps its price, the other two are replaced; (6) the field reads Pylon design ID.

Touches: `public/project.html` (step 3 Pylon card and BSTC block, pull handler, label, script tag, `?v=` bump), `public/js/pylon-equipment.js` (new: figures, lines, layout file and data sheets from `pylon_data`), `public/js/supabase-client.js` (`calculateBstcQuantity` next to `calculateStcQuantity`), `public/settings.html` (Battery STC factors card on the Terms tab, Battery models card, load and save), `public/quote.html` (layout image on Your system, link removed), `netlify/functions/pylon-sync.js` (file the layout image and any data sheets into the quote's documents), one migration (`battery_models` table, `company_settings.bstc_factors`), `docs/PROJECT_SPEC.md` A16 and the battery compliance doc.
Overlaps: none (6 Nov 2026: no unmerged branches; open PRs 297 docs and 34 email workers touch other files; item 59 touches `project-invoice-actuals.js` and the Terms tab of `settings.html`, so if 57 is built first this one adds its card below 57's).
Kind: feature into `staging`; migration yes; money yes (the BSTC count feeds the credit shown to the client, though the cap and claim functions do not change), stock no, RLS no, payroll no.
Size: medium to large (a day).
Best model: Fable 5.1, effort xhigh, because a migration and the client's credit figure ship together.

## 59. Invoice actual costs: pick a rate per clocked person, invoice notes written from the installers' notes, the terms note set in Settings (owner's yes 6 Nov 2026: built and shipped straight to `main`, migration 20261130010000; spec A22)

Owner, 6 Nov 2026: when invoicing actual costs, pick a different rate for the clocked in person; invoice notes written by AI from the installers' notes describing what was done, editable; and the 9% interest note on the invoice becomes an editable note set in Settings.

1. **Rate per person on clocked rows.** Every clocked row in the Invoice actual costs panel gets the same rate tier dropdown the "+ Add labour" rows already have, next to the person's name. Picking a tier reprices every clocked row of that person on this invoice (bands and the 2 hour minimum applied again), the $/hr box can still be typed over, and the invoice line names the tier picked. The server already checks the tier exists and prices the line (`_shared/invoice-items.js`), so no server change.
2. **Invoice notes.** A "Notes on the invoice" box in the panel with a "Write from the site notes" button: a new function reads the job's site visit notes (written at clock-off), job feed posts and completed task notes since the job's last invoice (the whole job for the first), plus the labour and material lines being billed, and writes 2 to 6 plain sentences on what was done (no prices, no rates). Edit it, then Create invoice saves it on the invoice (`invoices.notes`). The client's invoice and the PDF print it as "Work completed" above the terms. Every invoice card on the job gets a Notes button (any time, Xero or not: the notes never go to Xero) with the same Write button; Save remakes the PDF when there is one. "A separate invoice for each" puts the same note on each, editable per invoice afterwards.
3. **Terms note in Settings.** Settings > Terms (the tab with the payment details) gets an "Invoice terms note" box, filled with today's wording by the migration (the 9% pa sentence), saved by the same Save button. The invoice prints exactly what it says; blank prints nothing.

Migration (one): `invoices.notes text`, `company_settings.invoice_terms_text text` seeded with the current sentence, `get_invoice_by_token_v3` recreated to return both. No money, stock, RLS or payroll change (the invoices update policy already lets pricing roles save).

Done means: on staging (admin@staging.test, a do-and-charge job with clocked shifts) (1) Invoice actual costs > change one clocked row's tier to another tier: every row for that person reprices to that tier's $/hr times the band, the created invoice's line names the tier; (2) Write from the site notes fills the Notes box from the job's site notes, edit a word, Create invoice, open the client link: "Work completed" shows the edited text, the PDF too; (3) the invoice card's Notes button, change the text, Save: the client link and PDF show the new text; (4) Settings > Terms > Invoice terms note, change 9% to 10%, Save: the client link shows the new wording.

Touches: `public/project.html` (a Notes button and its listener on the invoice card, a script tag, `?v=` bumps), `public/settings.html` (one card on the Terms tab, load and save), `public/invoice.html` (notes block, terms from Settings), `public/js/project-invoice-actuals.js` (tier dropdown on clocked rows, Notes box), new `public/js/invoice-notes.js` (the Notes panel and the Write call, shared by the panel and the card), new `netlify/functions/generate-invoice-notes.js` (pricing roles, Claude Sonnet 5.5), `netlify/functions/create-invoice.js` (accepts `notes`), one migration, `docs/PROJECT_SPEC.md` A22. Overlaps: none (PRs 299 and 300 are merged into `staging`, no unmerged branches). Kind: feature into `staging`; migration yes; money, stock, RLS or payroll no. Size: medium (a morning). Test on staging: admin@staging.test; the Write button needs the Claude key in Settings > Integration keys on staging.
## 61. Invoices page as the accounts desk: status, history, and every action from the list (owner's yes 8 Nov 2026, built the same day straight to `main`)

Owner, 8 Nov 2026: invoices are created from the job, but checked, sent, chased, pushed and corrected from the Invoices page, usually by an accounts person who was not on the job. The whole process has to be easy to see: what stage each invoice is at, who did what and when.

1. **One history per invoice.** New table `invoice_events`: created, edited (old and new total), emailed to client, reminder sent, sent outside TEops, pushed to Xero, paid (Xero or the pay online link), credit note issued and pushed, notes changed. Written by the server where the server does the work (`_shared/invoice-events.js`), by the browser for the edit panel, the notes and Sent another way (pricing roles, actor is always the signed in person; `actor_label` when nobody did it). A trigger copies each line to the job's Activity tab, so one write per fact. Backfilled from `invoices` (created, paid, in Xero), online-approved deposits (sent) and the job activity log (emailed, edited, credit notes). `invoices.balance_due` becomes a computed column so every page reads the one balance.
2. **Status in plain words** (`js/invoice-status.js`, the same words on the job and on the Invoices page): Not sent (orange), Sent, awaiting payment, Overdue N days (red), Paid d Mon (green), Credited in full; plus an In Xero tick and the reminder dates.
3. **Invoices page rebuilt** (`invoices.html`): filters with counts (Needs sending, Awaiting payment, Overdue, Paid, Not in Xero, All), search, one row per invoice (invoice, job and client, amount, status, last action with the person's name, next step). Click a row for the invoice panel (`js/invoice-actions.js`): status, amount, due date, who created it, the timeline, and the buttons: Email to client, Send reminder (email filled in with the amount and due date), Push to Xero, Edit (not in Xero, not paid), Issue credit note (in Xero, finance and admin), Notes, View PDF, Sent another way, Open job. A button that cannot be used says why.
4. **The job's Invoices tab** shows the same status chip and last action on each invoice with an Open button for the same panel. The edit, push, credit note and email code moved out of `project.html` into `js/invoice-actions.js`, so the two pages can never disagree.

Not in this build: automatic reminders (still the item under "Payment reconciliation": drafts for approval after the bank is checked), marking paid by hand.

Done means (staging, admin@staging.test): (1) Invoices page, Needs sending: an unsent invoice shows Not sent; Email it: it moves to Awaiting payment and the timeline reads "Emailed to client" with your name and today; (2) Send reminder on an overdue invoice: the timeline shows the reminder, the email is on the job's Emails tab; (3) Edit an unpushed invoice from the panel: the list shows the new amount, the timeline shows Edited with the old and new totals; (4) Push to Xero from the panel: the In Xero tick shows, Edit greys out with its reason, Issue credit note appears as admin; (5) an invoice emailed before today shows that send in its timeline (backfill); (6) the job's Invoices tab shows the same status words and still creates invoices as before.

Touches: `public/invoices.html` (rebuilt), `public/js/invoice-actions.js` (new), `public/js/invoice-status.js` (new), `public/project.html` (invoice code moved out, chip and Open button, `?v=` bumps), `public/js/email-compose.js` (passes the invoice event), `netlify/functions/send-gmail.js`, `create-invoice.js`, `push-invoice-to-xero.js`, `create-credit-note.js`, `push-credit-note-to-xero.js`, `xero-webhook.js`, `airwallex-webhook.js`, `_shared/create-job-from-quote.js`, `_shared/invoice-events.js` (new), migration 20261130030000 (`invoice_events`, RLS, indexes, backfill), `docs/PROJECT_SPEC.md` A28. Overlaps: none (8 Nov 2026: only open PR is 34, email workers; no unmerged branch touches these files). Kind: feature with a migration, straight to `main` on a Sunday (weekend rule); money no (the edit logic moves, nothing changes how amounts are worked out), RLS yes (new table), stock and payroll no. Size: large (a day).

## 57. Stock kinds: cable by the drum, consumables and tools known by themselves, every receive counted (owner's yes 5 Nov 2026, built the same day, PR 296 into `staging`, LIVE 7 Nov 2026 straight to `main` on the owner's say (PR 311), see PROJECT_SPEC.md A12, A11b, A10)

Built in: `feat/stock-kinds-drums`. Production migration 20261130000000 applied by hand through the database tool on 6 and 7 Nov 2026 (backups `backup_20261105_*`): the old `material_supplier_price_set` signature is renamed `_material_supplier_price_set_old` there (not dropped), and `_po_line_take_back` and `po_create_stock_pull` run their deletes as dynamic SQL (the tool stops on a delete inside a function body), same behaviour; the next migration that rewrites them restores the plain text. Seven live items look like cable and carry a "Looks like cable, set it up?" chip. Test rows named "TEST Claude..." are left on the staging database: delete when convenient.

Owner (5 Nov 2026): a 100m drum of cable is bought as one thing and used 30m at a time; four drums with different amounts left are not "280m", a 50m run needs a drum with 50m on it; and the app should know cable, consumables and tools by itself the way it knows a bill. A read-only pass of Stock the same day found: gear received to the Warehouse or a van from a PO line with no Stock item was never counted (every line typed by name, and every line an emailed bill added to an open PO); scanning a part at clock-out added Took + Left on top of the count instead of setting it; a $0 item with a Buy from supplier set stayed at $0 when a different supplier billed it; Delete showed a raw database error; the stocktake never listed an item the app had at 0.

- **Kinds.** `materials.kind` part / cable / consumable, guessed from the name and category when nobody says (word rules in `_stock_kind_guess`, the same in `js/stock-kinds.js`, trigger `materials_kind_sync` on every insert): drum, coil, reel, roll or a length with a cable word means cable (drum size from the name, 100m when it does not say); bag of 100, 50pk, zip ties, saddles, screws, tape means consumable; drill, grinder, tester, Milwaukee, Makita means "looks like a tool" (a hint on the scan panel and on an unlinked PO line, never set by itself: tools go to the Tools register). The label reader (`part-label-ai.js`) also says the kind, the drum size and the pack size. Existing items keep their counts and prices: consumables stay consumables, everything else is a part, and one that looks like cable gets a "Looks like cable, set it up?" chip (its count and price so far are per drum; Edit, What kind of item = Cable converts them: count x drum size, cost and sell / drum size). The Stock edit panel has What kind of item with the drum size or pack box (`material_set_kind`, pricing roles).
- **Drums** (`material_units`, one row per drum: where, metres left, which PO line). Counts for cable stay in metres; `_stock_adjust` keeps the drums adding up to the count at each place (`_material_units_reconcile`), so a count typed as one number still leaves the drums right. Receiving a supplier PO line of cable makes one drum per unit ordered (`_po_line_land`); Undo takes full drums back and stops when one has been used (`_po_line_take_back`). Used stock on My Day: pick the drum (a full one, or the one with 60m on it), say what is left on it now or tap Finished it; the metres used go to the job and the drum keeps its reading (`po_create_stock_pull` with `unit_id`, `left_after`; the line remembers the drum and undo puts the metres back on it). Stocktake lists each drum with its metres plus "+ Drum" for one the app did not know; the van page and the Stock edit panel show every drum, tap one to set what is left (`material_unit_set`, `material_unit_add`); Move stock moves a drum (`stock_transfer_unit`). A PO line for a cable item is one drum at drum price; Suggest restock orders whole drums; the Stock page shows "280m: 2 full, 60m, 20m" per place.
- **Every receive counted.** A PO line with no Stock item received to the Warehouse or a van is linked to the item with that name, or added to Stock from the line (kind guessed, cost per metre for cable, Needs a price at $0), logged on the PO and the item (`_po_line_autolink`). Lines an emailed bill adds to an open PO carry the Stock item the bill line found by part number.
- **Prices.** A $0 item takes its first price from whoever bills it (Buy from stays as set). Cable is kept per metre: a bill line per drum is divided by the drum size (the line's unit, else its quantity, says which; the bill reader now reads the unit column); a price list line with no unit waits under Changes made ("Cable: per drum or per metre?").
- **Scan at clock-out** sets the count at that place (Took + Left is the whole count), a cable drum is one drum of that many metres. Delete says plainly when an item is on a PO or a job. The Stock page's "+ Add material" shows for pricing roles only and its PO card no longer lists the vans' POs.

Done means:
1. admin@staging.test, Stock page: an item named "2.5mm TPS twin and earth 100m" shows the chip "Cable: drum of 100m"; Edit shows What kind of item = Cable, 100 metres, and the drums per place.
2. Receive a PO line of 2 of that cable to the Warehouse: the Stock page shows "200m Warehouse (2 full)". Undo received: back to 0.
3. staff@staging.test, My Day, Used stock on a job: pick the cable, "A full drum", type 70 left: the Stock PO says 30m x the cable, the Stock page shows "170m Warehouse (1 full, 70m)".
4. Stocktake (Warehouse): the two drums are listed with their metres; type 60 on the part drum, Save: "1 count corrected", the page shows 160m.
5. Receive a PO line typed by name (not in Stock) to the Warehouse: it appears in Stock with that count and "Added to Stock when PO ... was received" in its log.
6. Scan a part into the van that is already there with Took 1, Left 4: the van count reads 5, not 10.

Touches: one migration 20261130000000 (`materials.kind`, `pack_qty`, `pack_unit`, `supplier_bill_line_items.unit`, `purchase_order_line_items.material_unit_id`, `material_units`, `_stock_kind_guess`, `materials_kind_sync`, `_material_units_reconcile`, `_stock_adjust`, `material_unit_set`, `material_unit_add`, `stock_transfer_unit`, `material_set_kind`, `_po_line_autolink`, `_po_line_land`, `_po_line_take_back`, `po_line_add_to_stock`, `po_create_stock_pull`, `po_undo_receive`, `stock_stocktake`, `material_add_from_scan`, `material_supplier_price_set` (new signature), `supplier_bill_price_lines`), `public/js/stock-kinds.js` (new), `public/js/stock-panels.js` (new: the Stocktake and Move panels moved out of `supabase-client.js`), `public/js/used-stock.js`, `public/js/stock-scan.js`, `public/js/supabase-client.js` (loaders, PO line row cable by the drum, tool hint), `public/stock.html`, `public/vehicle-stock.html`, `netlify/functions/_shared/part-label-ai.js`, `_shared/supplier-invoice-ai.js`, `_shared/supplier-bill-intake.js`, every page's `supabase-client.js?v=` bump. Overlaps: item 49 (waiting on yes) touches `supplier-bill-intake.js`, build 49 after this. Kind: feature into `staging`; migration yes; money and stock yes, RLS yes (`material_units`, read only, writes through functions), payroll no: backup before the production migration. Size: large. Test on staging: the six clicks above, admin and staff logins; the AI key on staging for the label reader. Best model: Fable 5.1, effort xhigh, because it changes stock counts, job costs and cost prices with a new table.

## 62. Pipeline moves done by the database (planned 9 Nov 2026, waiting on the owner's yes)

**What the check found (9 Nov 2026, production, read only).** The moves that happen in the database or on the server work: Ready to Book and Job Booked follow bookings and clock-ins (triggers, nightly sweep), a quote approval makes the job and the deposit, a paid deposit moves it on (J7057), a final claim moves Ready to Invoice to Awaiting Payment (J7056), Complete archives after 2 days. The moves that only run in the browser get skipped:

- **Awaiting Our Action with nothing open:** J7009, J7010, J7034 have no open required-before-scheduling task but sit at Awaiting Our Action. The gate (`refreshPipelineTaskGate`) only runs when a task is ticked on the job page, Tasks or My Day; a task deleted, made not required, or finished another way never re-runs it.
- **Client Handover with nothing left to do:** J7001, J7005, J7038, J7046 (direct jobs, no tasks at all) have sat at Client Handover since their clock-out "Complete". The handover check (`checkAndAdvanceHandover`) only runs when a handover task is ticked or the handover card is saved, so a job with no handover tasks never moves. J7012 was invoiced from Client Handover and stayed there.
- **Invoiced no-quote jobs never reach Awaiting Payment:** `create-invoice.js` only moves a job when the claim is "final", which needs a quoted total; a do-and-charge job ($0 quoted) is never final. J7012 and J7068 are invoiced and still at Client Handover and Ready to Invoice.
- **Paid only by webhook:** only the Xero and Airwallex webhooks set `paid_at` and run the all-paid check. SI3003 to SI3006 are not in Xero, so their jobs can never reach Complete by themselves. PR 316 (item 61) does not add mark paid by hand either.
- **Jobs in quote columns:** the board offers every column to a job, so J7036 sits at Quote Sent and J7037 at Lead (ServiceM8 imports moved by hand). J7031 is at Awaiting Payment with no invoice in the app (invoiced in ServiceM8). Board moves are not written to the activity log, so there is no trail.
- By design and left alone: Job Not Complete is hand-moved only (J7004 since 22 Oct, J7014 and J7051 have people booked or clocked in).

**Plan.** One migration moves the three browser-only checks into the database so every route (page, board, server, SQL, a future mark-paid button) lands a job in the right column, and each automatic move writes an activity line ("Moved to Ready to Book: no required tasks open", `changed_by` null):

1. `project_handover_ready(p_project_id)`: the same rule as `checkAndAdvanceHandover` (no open handover task; solar also NMI, panel and inverter serials and a Formbay status; battery NMI and Formbay).
2. `job_tasks_sync_stage` trigger (after insert, update, delete on `job_tasks`): the job at Awaiting Our Action or Ready to Book takes `project_unbooked_stage`; at Client Handover it moves to Ready to Invoice when `project_handover_ready`.
3. `projects_stage_rules` before-update trigger on `projects`: a job entering Client Handover, or one at Client Handover whose NMI, serials or Formbay status change, moves on to Ready to Invoice when ready (so the clock-out "Complete" on a direct job lands at Ready to Invoice).
4. `invoices_sync_complete` trigger (after update of `paid_at` on `invoices`): a job at Awaiting Payment with every invoice paid moves to Complete, whichever way the invoice was marked paid.
5. `create-invoice.js`: an invoice on a job with no quoted total raised while the job is at Client Handover or Ready to Invoice moves it to Awaiting Payment (it is the invoice for the work); a final claim raised from Client Handover moves it too. Job Not Complete stays put (work still going).
6. `dashboard.html`: Lead, Draft Quote and Quote Sent offered to quotes only, and a board move writes an activity line.
7. Delete `checkAndAdvanceHandover` and `refreshPipelineTaskGate` and their calls (`project.html`, `tasks.html`, `my-day.html`, `assets.js`): the triggers do the work. The clock-out message says which column the job landed in.
8. One-off in the migration: J7009, J7010, J7034 to Ready to Book; J7001, J7005, J7038, J7046 to Ready to Invoice; J7012 and J7068 to Awaiting Payment; J7036 and J7037 to Ready to Book. J7031 is left for the owner (Complete, or Archived).

**Done means:** (1) tick the last required task on a job at Awaiting Our Action from the Tasks page: the board shows it at Ready to Book with an activity line; (2) delete that task instead: same result; (3) clock out of a direct job with no tasks and tap Complete: the job lands at Ready to Invoice; (4) raise an invoice on a no-quote job at Ready to Invoice: it lands at Awaiting Payment; (5) set `paid_at` on that invoice by SQL on staging: it lands at Complete; (6) a job card on the board no longer offers Lead, Draft Quote or Quote Sent.

Touches: one migration (`job_tasks_sync_stage`, `projects_stage_rules`, `invoices_sync_complete`, `project_handover_ready`, the 11 job moves), `public/js/supabase-client.js` (delete two functions, clock-out message, `?v=` bump on every page), `public/project.html`, `public/tasks.html`, `public/my-day.html`, `public/js/assets.js`, `public/dashboard.html`, `netlify/functions/create-invoice.js` (the stage block only), `docs/PROJECT_SPEC.md` A6. Overlaps: PR 316 (item 61, open) touches `create-invoice.js`, `project.html` and both webhooks; build this after 316 merges, or in the same chat; no unmerged branches on `staging`; nothing waiting on yes touches these. Kind: bug fix (jobs not moving is wrong on live) into `main`, then `main` into `staging`; migration yes; money no (stage only), stock no, RLS no, payroll no. Size: medium (a morning). Test on staging: admin@staging.test, the six Done means clicks; no keys needed. Best model: Fable 5.1, effort xhigh, because it is a migration with triggers on the live jobs table plus code in one go.

## 63. One-off items on a do-and-charge invoice (owner's yes 9 Nov 2026, built the same day straight to `main`; spec A22)

Owner, 9 Nov 2026: when creating an invoice, something used on the job is sometimes not in Stock and does not need adding to Stock (bought for this job only). Today the only ways onto the invoice are a Stock material, a prebuild or a PO line received to the job.

1. **"+ One-off item"** in each cost centre's materials section of the Invoice actual costs panel (`js/project-invoice-actuals.js`), beside "+ Add from Stock" and "+ Add prebuild": description (required), quantity (1 by default), price each ex GST (typed), and an optional cost each so the job's margin stays right. The row sits with the other material rows, editable, with the same Bill tick, and prints on the client's invoice as its own line (description, quantity, price) like any item. It posts to Xero inside the stage's Materials total. Nothing moves in Stock and nothing is added to Stock.
2. **Server** (`_shared/invoice-items.js`, `create-invoice.js`): a new line kind `custom`: priced from the typed price (no Stock or prebuild lookup), `unit_cost` from the typed cost or 0, `material_amount` = quantity x price. Description required, price and quantity not negative. Recorded in `invoice_line_items` like the rest, so the next invoice does not offer it again and item 64 can edit it.
3. **Migration:** widen the `invoice_line_items.kind` check to `material, prebuild, labour, custom`. Nothing else.

Done means (staging, admin@staging.test, a do-and-charge job): (1) Invoice actual costs > + One-off item, "Hire of scissor lift", 1, $350: the row shows with the Bill tick, the panel total goes up by $350; (2) Create invoice: the client's invoice page prints "Hire of scissor lift 1 x $350.00"; (3) Stock is unchanged (no draw, no new material); (4) the Xero breakdown on the invoice shows $350 more under Materials.

Touches: `public/js/project-invoice-actuals.js` (the button, the row, the payload; `?v=` bump in `project.html`), `netlify/functions/_shared/invoice-items.js` (kind `custom`), `netlify/functions/create-invoice.js` (accepts it), one migration (the kind check), `docs/PROJECT_SPEC.md` A22. Overlaps: item 64 (same panel, build 63 first in the same chat); item 62 touches `create-invoice.js` too (its stage move), different lines. Kind: feature (into `staging`, a weekday); migration yes (a check constraint); money yes (what the client is charged, typed by a person, checked on the server), stock no, RLS no, payroll no. Size: small (under a morning). Test on staging: admin@staging.test, J9002 or a new no-quote job, the four clicks above. Best model: Opus 5.5, effort high, because it is a known shape (one more item kind through the same pricing path) with a one-line migration.

## 64. Edit a do-and-charge invoice with its lines before it reaches Xero (owner's yes 9 Nov 2026, built the same day straight to `main`; spec A22)

Owner, 9 Nov 2026: an invoice is created, then it turns out not all the POs were in. Once the POs are received, the choice today is a second invoice, or Edit on the first, which shows only the numbers per stage, not the shifts and materials the create panel showed. Wanted: Edit on an unpushed do-and-charge invoice reopens the full lines and the newly arrived materials, and saves into the same invoice.

1. **Edit reopens the Invoice actual costs panel for that invoice** (from the invoice panel, item 61, and the job tab). It shows the invoice's own lines first, ticked and editable: every clocked shift, material, Stock or prebuild item, one-off item (item 63) and the Less line, plus the notes. Underneath, everything on the job not yet on any invoice (a PO line received since, a new shift) with the Bill tick on, so nothing arrived is missed; a tick off leaves it for the next invoice as today. Quantities, prices and descriptions can be changed; a line can be removed. The quoted (claim %) invoices keep the numbers-only Edit.
2. **Save, all or nothing.** Prices come from the server as on create (`priceInvoiceItems`); then one database function `edit_invoice_lines(p_invoice_id, p_lines jsonb, p_sent_at, p_due_date, p_notes)` (pricing roles, refuses an invoice in Xero or paid) applies the whole set in one transaction: removed Stock items go back to the warehouse (`_stock_adjust`), removed shifts and materials become unbilled again (`time_entries.status`, `invoice_id`, `job_material_usage` marks), added lines are recorded and their shifts and materials marked billed, Stock taken out for new Stock items, the claim and invoice totals recomputed, the stage's `invoiced_amount` moved by the difference, the PDF dropped so the next email remakes it, and one history line "Edited: Total ex GST $a to $b, 2 lines added, 1 removed, 1 changed" (invoice_events, item 61) which the trigger copies to the job's Activity tab. The browser never writes money tables for this.
3. **No accidental second invoice:** when a job already has an unpushed, unpaid do-and-charge invoice, the create panel's Create button offers "Add to SI123 instead" (opens the edit above with the new lines ticked) next to "Make a new invoice".
4. **Xero after:** an invoice already in Xero still cannot be edited (Xero rejects line changes on an approved invoice); the panel keeps saying so and points to a credit note or a new invoice.

Done means (staging, admin@staging.test, a do-and-charge job with two shifts and one received PO line): (1) Invoice actual costs > Create invoice with the shifts only; (2) receive a second PO line to the job; (3) Invoices page > the invoice > Edit: the two shifts show ticked, the new PO line shows underneath ticked; change one shift's hours, Save: the invoice total and the client's invoice page show the shift change and the new material, Stock and the job's costs unchanged for the PO line, the history reads "Edited by Test Admin: Total ex GST $a to $b, 1 line added, 1 changed"; (4) untick the PO line and Save again: it is offered on the next Create invoice; (5) Push to Xero, then Edit: still off with its reason; (6) Create invoice on the same job with the first invoice unpushed: "Add to SI... instead" is offered.

Touches: `public/js/project-invoice-actuals.js` (edit mode: load the invoice's lines, merge with the unbilled, Save calls the new function; `?v=` bump), `public/js/invoice-actions.js` (Edit on an invoice with line items opens the actuals panel; the numbers-only panel stays for quoted claims), `public/project.html` (the create panel's "Add to SI... instead"), `netlify/functions/update-invoice-lines.js` (new: pricing roles, prices the lines, calls the function), `netlify/functions/get-invoiceable-actuals.js` (also returns the lines already on a given invoice), `netlify/functions/_shared/invoice-items.js`, one migration (`edit_invoice_lines(...)`, security definer, `is_pricing_role()`, revoke from public and anon), `docs/PROJECT_SPEC.md` A22. Overlaps: item 63 (same panel, same chat, 63 first); item 62 (`create-invoice.js` stage move, different lines, and its triggers on `invoices.paid_at` do not fire on an edit); item 61 live (the invoice panel's Edit button is the door). Kind: feature (into `staging`); migration yes; money yes, stock yes (Stock items in and out), RLS no new table (one security definer function), payroll no; backup of `invoices`, `invoice_claims`, `invoice_line_items`, `job_material_usage` and `time_entries` before the production migration. Size: large (a day). Test on staging: admin@staging.test, the six clicks above; no outside keys needed (Xero push can stay unmade; a fake `xero_invoice_id` by SQL covers click 5). Best model: Fable 5.1, effort xhigh, because it moves money and stock in one database function and reopens the create panel on saved data.

## 65. Jobs page in stage folders (owner's yes 9 Nov 2026, "build straight to main", LIVE the same day, spec A6)

Owner, 9 Nov 2026: "On the job page have sub folders like quote do with the stage the job is in." The Quotes page sorts quotes into tabs (Active, Sent to Client, Approved, Declined, Archived) with a count on each. The Jobs page (`projects.html`) is one flat table of every job except archived, with a search box. On 9 Nov 2026 live has 69 jobs: 37 at Ready to Book, 12 at Job Not Complete, 8 at Client Handover, 5 at Awaiting Our Action, 2 at Awaiting Payment, 2 archived, and one each at Lead, Quote Sent, Awaiting Client Action, Job Booked and Ready to Invoice.

1. **A row of folder tabs** above the jobs table, the same tabs as the Quotes page (`settings-tabs`): **All** first and open by default, then one tab per pipeline stage in board order (`PIPELINE_STAGES`, the labels the board uses), then **Archived** last. Every tab shows its count. A stage with no jobs is left out so the row stays short; on a phone the row scrolls sideways as on Quotes.
2. **The table, the search box and the four money cards follow the open folder:** click Ready to Book and the table shows those 37 jobs and the cards total them; type in the search and it narrows within the folder. Dev Mode select-all and delete work on the rows shown, as today.
3. **Archived jobs come back into view** in their own folder (they are hidden from the page entirely today; the board's "(Archived ->)" link is the only way to see them). They stay out of All and out of the money cards unless the Archived folder is open. The query drops its `neq archived` filter and the grouping happens in the browser; no second query.
4. **The folder you were on is remembered** on the device (`localStorage`, `te-jobs-folder`), so an accounts person who lives in Awaiting Payment lands there next time. A remembered stage that has no jobs any more falls back to All.
5. The row of counts refreshes with the list after a delete, and the stage label on each tab comes from `pipelineStageLabel()` so a renamed stage renames the folder.

**Done means** (staging, admin@staging.test, Jobs page): (1) a row of tabs sits above the table: All (n), then the stages that have jobs in board order, then Archived; (2) click Ready to Book: only those jobs show and the four cards total them; (3) type a client name in the search: the list narrows within that folder; (4) click Archived: the archived jobs show, and they are not in All; (5) reload the page: it opens on the folder you left; (6) Dev Mode on, tick two jobs in a folder, Delete selected: the counts on the tabs drop.

Touches: `public/projects.html` only (the tab row, the filter, the remembered folder; no new inline code elsewhere), `docs/PROJECT_SPEC.md` (the Jobs page paragraph). Overlaps: PR 319 (job PO share) bumps the `supabase-client.js?v=` line in `projects.html`, one line, merges clean; no other unmerged branch on `staging` and nothing waiting on yes touches `projects.html` (item 47, job merge, hides merged rows there, not yet yes). Kind: feature (into `staging`, a weekday); migration no; money no (display only), stock no, RLS no, payroll no. Size: small (under an hour). Test on staging: admin@staging.test, Jobs page, the six clicks above; no keys needed. Best model: Sonnet 5.5, effort medium, because it is a one-file front-end change copying a pattern the Quotes page already has.

## 69. simPRO quote into a TEops job (idea 9 Nov 2026, waiting on the owner's yes; was numbered 66 for a few hours, 66 is the interest item)

Owner, 9 Nov 2026: "Is there a way to get a quote out of simPRO and fill out a job in TEops with the quote data?" Yes. TEops already does this for ServiceM8 (spec A27): a review screen shows each quote or job as it would come across (client match, site, name, lines, quoted amount), the admin ticks the rows, and `sm8-import-jobs.js` makes the job the same way an approved quote does (one stage, labour lines on the matching rate tier at sell, materials at cost, the markup worked out so the stage sells for exactly the quoted figure, `stageFromLines`). A simPRO import is that same screen fed from simPRO instead. Two ways in, cheapest first:

1. **A file from simPRO (no simPRO setup):** on the quote in simPRO, print or export it (the quote PDF, or the items spreadsheet if simPRO gives one with cost and sell) and drop the file on a new Settings > simPRO import screen (admin). Claude reads it the way the supplier invoice reader does (`_shared/supplier-invoice-ai.js` pattern: client, site, quote number, every section or cost centre, each line with quantity, cost, sell, labour or material), the review screen shows it with "AI read, check" on every line, the admin fixes anything wrong, picks the client (matched by name, or new) and the site, then presses Make job. A client PDF usually shows sell only, so materials come across at sell with 0% markup (as the ServiceM8 import does when there is no cost) unless the file has costs.
2. **simPRO's API (later, once simPRO is settled on):** simPRO has a REST API on each build (`https://<build>.simprosuite.com/api/v1.0`, OAuth2; turned on inside simPRO, key kept in Settings > API Keys like `servicem8` and `pylon`). The screen then lists simPRO quotes (last N months, like the ServiceM8 one) with their real sections, cost centres and items at cost and sell, so nothing is read by eye. One quote can take several calls (quote, then its cost centres, then each item type), so the screen loads lines a few quotes at a time as the ServiceM8 one does. Not verified against simPRO's own API reference this session (the cloud session cannot reach it); check `developer.simprogroup.com` before building.

Either way the job that lands is a normal TEops job: Quoted type (claims against the quoted amount, Invoice project), stage lines ready for POs and Stock, Ready to Book, with the simPRO quote number on the job so a re-run skips it and the Summary says where it came from. If the quote is not yet accepted it can land as a TEops Draft quote instead (tick on the review screen), approved the usual way. simPRO is a quoting tool here only: nothing is written back to simPRO, and TEops owns the job from then on.

To decide before building: whether the quotes will keep being written in simPRO at all (TEops quotes have prebuilds, commercial agreements and the proposal PDF; simPRO's estimating and takeoff are the reason to keep it for big commercial jobs), and which simPRO export the owner can actually produce (a quick screenshot of simPRO's print and export options on one quote answers it).

**Done means** (staging, admin@staging.test, Settings > simPRO import): (1) drop a simPRO quote PDF on the screen: the client, site, quote number and every line show within a minute, each tagged "AI read, check"; (2) fix one quantity and untag it, pick the client, press Make job: a J number opens at Ready to Book with one stage whose total equals the simPRO quote ex GST; (3) the stage's lines show labour on a rate tier and materials with quantity and cost; (4) drop the same PDF again: the screen says it is already J####; (5) Invoice project on that job offers the quoted amount.

Touches (way 1): new `public/simpro-import.html` (copied from `sm8-import.html`, the review table and Make job), new `netlify/functions/simpro-import-extract.js` (admin, Claude reads the file), new `agents/prompts/simpro-quote.js` (the reading prompt), `netlify/functions/_shared/sm8-import.js` (the job-from-lines part moved to `_shared/job-from-lines.js` so both imports share it; `sm8-import-jobs.js` keeps working), `public/settings.html` (one link under Admin, no inline code), one migration (`projects.simpro_quote_number`, a column and an index, so a re-run skips a quote already brought across), `docs/PROJECT_SPEC.md` (A27 gains a simPRO paragraph). Way 2 adds `netlify/functions/simpro-import-preview.js` and `simpro-import-lines.js` and the `simpro` key in Settings > API Keys. Overlaps: none (no unmerged branch or open PR touches `sm8-import.html` or `_shared/sm8-import.js`; `settings.html` gains one link only). Kind: feature (into `staging`); migration yes (one column); money no (a job's quoted figure is set, nothing is invoiced), stock no, RLS no, payroll no. Size: medium (a morning for way 1; way 2 a day once the simPRO key exists). Test on staging: admin@staging.test, the five clicks above; needs the Anthropic key on staging (already there for the invoice reader). Best model: Opus 5.5, effort high, because it is a feature build that reuses an existing import path and an existing AI reader; nothing touches money or RLS.

## 70. Simpler quote form (owner's yes 10 Nov 2026, "build, push it straight to main", LIVE the same day, spec A3)

**As built (10 Nov 2026):** points 1 to 8 as planned, with these calls made in the build:
- The four figures typed twice (battery kWh, solar price, battery price, Show the savings estimate) stayed on step 1's bill card and left step 3, not the other way round: the bill card saves itself and holds the savings estimate they drive, step 3 needs Save pricing.
- Solar is 9 questions (the plan said 10: merging the two existing system questions took one more off). Battery is 11; a Solar quote with Batteries as well asks 10 more.
- The brief does not copy the notes: Generate sends the Internal notes with the brief, so nothing is typed twice.
- Answers already given to the retired questions (access notes, switchboard, existing system and the rest) still show on the job, My Day and to the AI, under their old labels (`RETIRED_QUESTION_LABELS` in `template-questions.js`); step 1 no longer asks them.
- The proposal adds the deposit row only when the quote's stages do not already make a whole schedule (a leading "Deposit" row, or stages adding up to 100% alone). All 9 production quotes with stages had their own deposit row on 10 Nov 2026, so none changed.
- Lead files are copied after the quote is made; any that fail are named in the quote's activity.
- Left as they were: `saveEdits`' quote branches (not certain only jobs reach that editor), `_shared/pdf-cover-page.js`' cover photo branch (no setting feeds it now; a weekly cleanup can take it out), the job editor's own Default markup box.
- Point 9 (production data): the "Electricity bill" question and its photo item on the "Solar Quote - Documents to Gather" checklist. The Supabase tool stopped for a confirmation nobody answered, so it waits on one Allow tap; backup schema `backup_20261110` is made in the same statement.
- Proof: the staging site and database are blocked from cloud sessions (item 36's note), so the page was run in a local browser with stubbed data: no page errors; before and after screenshots on `pr-assets`.

Owner's ask (10 Nov 2026): "have a look over the quoting form and simplify it, check all the templates for stuff that is unused now or legacy and make them simple to use." Two read-only passes over `new-project.html`, the Quote tab in `project.html`, `template-questions.js`, the proposal page, the functions and the settings, plus a count on production (11 quotes carry answers; 0 rows are `time_and_materials`; `direct_job` is the no-quote job type, not a template; the site inspection checklist already asks for the switchboard, meter and NMI, roof, existing solar and battery location).

What is wrong today, worst first:
1. Step 1 asks 36 questions on a solar and battery quote. 12 are read by nothing (`roof_condition`, `storeys`, `shading`, `access`, `distance_to_board`, `walls_open`, `battery_kwh_wanted`, `switchboard_type` twice, `daily_usage`, `info_date`, `info_by`, `info_notes`), 3 duplicate the Internal notes and the step 4 brief, 3 are known at Create (where it came from, when, who), 2 overlap each other (`existing_system` and `existing_pv`), and 6 required ones only feed a display line yet block sending (`grid_type`, `roof_type`, `phases`, `coupling`, `battery_location`, `switchboard_type`).
2. Four figures are typed in two places, step 1's bill card and step 3 (show savings, battery kWh, solar price, battery price), kept in step by mirror code.
3. Dead boxes: Default $ per STC and zone (hidden `display:none`, never shown), Default markup % (hard-coded 45, ignores Settings, not saved), Estimate wording (copied only at Create, blank on a type change; the proposal then uses its own hard-coded text, not Settings), Scope style (never saved), Settings Default deposit % (nothing reads it), Settings Cover page photo (never drawn: the proposal cover is hard-coded per type).
4. Pre-quote information shows three times on a quote (step 1, Summary, Details); Details has a second Notes box that overwrites the Quote card's, and a legacy "Needs a site visit" tick that never ticks step 2; step 1 and step 2 each find the linked inspection.
5. Changing the proposal type on step 1 swaps only the cover photos: `applyTemplatePaymentDefaults` is never called, so a New Build switched to Solar keeps 10% and no milestones (spec says the type sets them). Solar and Battery default milestones carry their own "Deposit 10%" row that can disagree with Deposit %.
6. Promised but not built: lead attachments never reach the scope of works (`sow_documents` starts empty), and the AI never reads Internal notes though Create says it does.
7. Manual steps the app knows the answer to: step 2 ignores the lead's "Needs a site visit" and a linked inspection; step 5 "Check now" runs the same check Email runs; "Generate PDF" when every save already remakes it; five Save buttons while the bill card autosaves.
8. Legacy: `time_and_materials` (no creator, 0 rows) still branched on in `quote.html`, `project.html`, `create-invoice.js`, `create-job-from-quote.js` and the Settings text; `TEMPLATE_INFO` defined twice with different wording; `saveEdits` quote branches dead; wording ("Cost Centres tab", "quote pages").

Plan (feature into `staging`, no migration):
1. **Step 1, fewer questions, filled by itself.** Drop the 12 unread questions and `info_notes`; merge `existing_system` and detail into `existing_pv` on both types. Create fills `info_source` (Lead form, Client email, else the person picks), and the date and who come from `created_at` and `created_by`. Required stays only where a rule or a task reads it: `with_battery` (defaulted Yes when Pylon has storage or a battery is quoted), `life_support` (a Yes adds a pre-job task "Life support on site: tell the DNSP"), `backup_needs`, `location_clear`, `vpp_capable`, `cec_listed`, `first_battery`, `solar_commissioned_first`, `existing_pv` on Battery. `grid_type`, `roof_type`, `phases`, `coupling`, `battery_location` stay as optional (one line each on "What the customer gets"). `nmi`, `dnsp`, `phases` keep filling from the bill reading; the NMI answer is copied to `projects.nmi` at approval so the handover rule and the job do not ask again. Old answers stay in `template_answers`; dropped ones just stop showing.
2. **One copy of every figure.** Show savings, battery kWh, solar price and battery price live on step 3 only; the step 1 bill card keeps the bill upload and reading. The mirror code goes.
3. **Dead boxes out.** Remove the hidden STC defaults (the legacy per-stage block defaults to 35 and zone 1.382), Default markup % (new stages take `company_settings.default_markup_percent`), Estimate wording (the proposal prints Settings' default when the quote's is blank), Scope style, Subtitle (the proposal already falls back to the scope intro), Settings Default deposit % and the Cover page photo card (the Photo Categories help text says what the photos really do: the inside pages).
4. **One card per thing.** Summary and Details stop showing Pre-quote information and the Notes box on a step quote; the Details "Needs a site visit" tick goes; step 2 owns the inspection link.
5. **The type sets its money defaults.** Save answers with a new type applies that type's deposit, milestones and wording where the quote still has the old type's defaults (untouched values only). The "Deposit 10%" row leaves the default milestones; the proposal always prints Deposit % first, then the milestones.
6. **What was promised.** Create copies the lead's attachments into the scope documents; Generate sends Internal notes with the brief, and the brief defaults to the notes.
7. **Steps that tick themselves.** Create sets step 2 to Needed when the lead said so; a linked inspection sets Needed, a completed one ticks the step. The ready-to-send check runs when the quote opens and after each save (its result sits on step 5; "Check now" goes). "Generate PDF" goes, View PDF stays. Save buttons stay this build (autosave on five cards is a bigger change; the bill card's pattern can follow later).
8. **Legacy out.** `time_and_materials` branches and the Settings text removed (the check constraint keeps the value until the next projects migration); `TEMPLATE_INFO` once, in `template-questions.js`; `applyTemplatePaymentDefaults` now used; dead `saveEdits` quote branches and stale wording fixed; the deposit rule found by id, not array index.
9. **Production data (owner's yes, same time):** the "Electricity bill" item leaves the "Solar Quote - Documents to Gather" checklist (step 1's bill card already files it).

## 66. Late payment interest per invoice (owner's yes 9 Nov 2026, built and live the same day, PR 331)

Owner, 9 Nov 2026: a toggle on the invoice so that, when it is turned on for that invoice, the interest accrued shows and the overdue reminder carries the interest charge; worked out so it reconciles cleanly in Xero even though it accrues daily and a client may pay a day or two after the notice; if they pay without the interest, either turn it off or chase the interest on its own. Per invoice, never for everyone.

1. **Charge interest** (invoice panel, off by default, this invoice only): on, the panel shows "Interest accrued: $X to today: N days at $D a day" (simple interest, from the day after the due date, on the balance owing less credit notes, at Settings > Payment Terms & T&Cs > Late payment interest %, 21 from the T&Cs). The Invoices page shows "+ $X interest accruing" under the amount and the Next column says so. On and off are history lines (copied to the job's Activity tab).
2. **Reminder with interest:** Send reminder adds "Under our terms, interest accrues on overdue amounts at 21% per annum, calculated daily. On this invoice it is $X to today (N days) and $D for each further day, so $T clears it if paid today. The interest is invoiced separately." The client's invoice page shows the same accrual under the totals while unpaid.
3. **Raise interest invoice:** a separate invoice "Interest on SI123: N days to d Mon yyyy at 21% pa" for the fixed amount, GST free, same job, numbered like any invoice, Push to Xero to the Interest charged account (Settings > Xero Mapping, 270 Interest Income, BAS excluded, change it there). SI123's accrual restarts the day after, so a top-up only covers new days. The original invoice and its Xero figure never change.
4. **Paid without the interest:** the accrual stops at the payment date; the panel shows it with Raise interest invoice (chase it as its own invoice) and Waive (turn off, history line "Interest waived $X").
5. One rule for the sum: `interest_days`, `interest_daily` and `interest_accrued` are computed columns on `invoices`, read by the Invoices page, the panel, the reminder, the client's page and `raise-interest-invoice.js`.

Not built: automatic reminders or automatic charging, compounding, a per-invoice rate (the toggle can carry one later if wanted), cancelling an interest invoice from TEops (if one is wrong, void it in Xero; Raise asks before it makes one).

Done means (staging, admin@staging.test): (1) Invoices > SI9101 (overdue) > Charge interest on: the accrual line shows days at the daily figure, history "Interest charging turned on", the list row shows "+ $X interest accruing"; (2) Send reminder: the email quotes the interest to today, the daily figure and the total if paid today; (3) Raise interest invoice: a new invoice "Interest on SI9101: N days to d Mon at 21% pa" appears, GST free, with Push to Xero, SI9101's accrual restarts; (4) Waive: history "Interest waived $X", the toggle is off, the row stops showing interest; (5) Settings > Late payment interest %, 21 to 20: the accrual changes to match.

Touches: `public/js/invoice-actions.js` (toggle, accrual, Raise and Waive, reminder words), `public/js/invoice-status.js` (interest figures and labels), `public/invoices.html` (row note), `public/invoice.html` (accrued line, the interest invoice's one line, INVOICE when no GST), `public/settings.html` (the rate), `public/js/supabase-client.js` (Interest charged group in the Xero breakdown; the Stock picker moved to `js/stock-picker.js` so `settings.html` stays under its budget), `netlify/functions/raise-interest-invoice.js` (new), `netlify/functions/_shared/build-xero-invoice-payload.js` (the interest line), migration 20261130050000 (`late_interest_rate_pct`, `invoices.interest_*`, the three computed columns, `get_invoice_balance_due` counts `interest_amount` GST free, `get_invoice_by_token_v3` returns the figures, the `interest` Xero mapping row, the history kinds and labels), `docs/PROJECT_SPEC.md` A22 and H, `docs/BUSINESS_FACTS.md`. Overlaps: item 62 (planned) would hold a job at Awaiting Payment while an interest invoice is unpaid, which is right. Kind: feature with a migration; money and Xero yes, stock no, RLS no new table (computed columns and a server-only path), payroll no; backups of `invoices`, `company_settings` and `xero_account_mapping` before the production migration. Size: large (a day).

## 67. Quoted lines on a claim invoice (owner's yes 9 Nov 2026, built and live the same day)

**As built (9 Nov 2026):** as planned. Migration 20261130060000 (`invoices.show_quote_lines` default true, existing rows false; `cost_centre_quote_lines()` prices the stage's lines as the quote page does; `get_invoice_by_token_v3` returns them; two history kinds). J7011's SI3010 set on at release. The lines' sum can differ from the claim by a cent or two (quote rounding, the same as the quote page); the "This claim" line is what is charged.

Owner, 9 Nov 2026, on J7011: SI3010 "still has the old style invoice, I think it's cause it's a quote. Can we make it toggleable so we can show line items rather than the description only." Right: SI3010 was raised as a claim against the quoted stage (Invoice next claim, 100% of Labour & Materials), and a claim prints the stage as one line because it has no lines of its own; a do and charge invoice prints its lines because it has them. The stage's quoted lines exist (`cost_centre_line_items`: 24v lithium battery, WP stainless enclosure, Sundries, licensed Electrician 6.5 hrs, Apprentice Electrician 6.5 hrs, which price to the claimed $2,760.24).

Plan:
1. **Every claim invoice raised from now on lists its quoted lines by itself** (what the client was quoted is what they are billed for): `invoices.show_quote_lines` defaults to true for new invoices; invoices raised before this ships keep the one line so a PDF already sent still matches the page. One tick on the invoice panel, "Quoted lines", turns it off for one invoice or on for an older one (J7011's SI3010 at release, with the owner's yes); a history line records either (`quote_lines_on` / `quote_lines_off`). Deposits, do and charge invoices and interest invoices are not touched. Any time, in Xero or not: it changes what the client sees, never the amounts.
2. On, the client's invoice page and PDF list each stage's quoted lines under the stage name: description, quantity, price, the same lines and prices the quote page shows (materials at the stage markup, labour at the quoted rate, a prebuild as one line with its client description), then the stage value. A partial claim keeps the lines in full and adds one line "This claim: X% of this stage" so the section still totals what is claimed; a 100% claim just lists the lines. Off, the invoice is as today.
3. Xero is untouched (labour and materials by the mapping), `balance_due` and the Claim summary are untouched.

Done means (staging, admin@staging.test): (1) J9002 > Cost Centres > Invoice next claim: the new invoice's Client's invoice page lists the stage's quoted lines and the stage value with no tick pressed, the total unchanged; (2) Invoices > SI9101 (raised before) still shows one line; tick Quoted lines and the lines appear; untick, back to one line; (3) Regenerate PDF shows the lines; (4) a partial claim shows the lines plus "This claim: X% of this stage"; (5) History shows "Quoted lines shown" and "Quoted lines hidden" with who and when. On live, SI3010 is set on at release and J7011's five lines appear.

Touches: `public/invoice.html` (the lines under each stage), `public/js/invoice-actions.js` (the tick and its history, `?v=4`), `public/js/invoice-status.js` (two labels), one migration (`invoices.show_quote_lines` default true, existing rows false, `get_invoice_by_token_v3` returns each stage's quoted lines priced as the quote does, the two history kinds; at release SI3010 set on), `docs/PROJECT_SPEC.md` A22. Overlaps: item 68 (same files), build both in this chat one after the other; nothing on `staging`; open PR 34 is the agents. Kind: feature (into `staging`, or straight to main on the owner's word); migration yes; money no (amounts unchanged, presentation only), stock no, RLS no, payroll no. Size: small (2 to 3 hours). Test on staging: admin@staging.test, the five clicks above, no keys needed. Simpler: nobody ticks anything on a new claim, the lines are just there; the one tick left is for hiding them on one invoice or showing them on an older one, which cannot be automatic because a PDF already sent must keep matching its page. Best model: Fable 5.1, effort xhigh, because it is a migration plus code in one go (the client's invoice function).

## 68. Interest in the client's totals and in Pay online (owner's yes 9 Nov 2026, built and live the same day)

**As built (9 Nov 2026):** points 1 to 5 as planned, no migration. The interest invoice a card payment makes is pushed to Xero when the original is in Xero; if that push fails, a history line on the interest invoice says so and it shows Not in Xero on the Invoices page for Push to Xero by hand. Point 6 (a Settings default so Charge interest starts on) was offered, not built: owner to say. The raise logic moved to `netlify/functions/_shared/interest-invoice.js` and the Xero push core to `_shared/push-invoice-to-xero.js`, both shared with the webhook.

Owner, 9 Nov 2026: "on the invoice near the GST figure should be interest, and when they tick pay online it shows the updated amount each day." Today (item 66) the client's invoice shows "Interest accrued to today" as a note under Balance Due, and Pay online charges the balance only; the interest is charged by hand with Raise interest invoice.

Plan (no migration: the figures already come from the computed columns and `get_invoice_by_token_v3`):
1. **Client's invoice page and PDF**, when Charge interest is on and the invoice is unpaid: the totals block gains two lines under Balance Due, "Late payment interest to d Mon (N days at R% pa, GST free)" and **"Total to pay today"** (balance plus interest). The phone summary card and the Pay online button show the total to pay today. Off, or paid: as today. The PDF carries the figure as at the day it was made, with that date in the label.
2. **Pay online** (`get-or-create-payment-link.js`): the amount is the balance plus the interest accrued at the moment of the click, so it moves each day; the link is remade whenever the amount changes (it already is when the balance changes). Nothing else about the payment changes.
3. **A card payment that includes interest** (`airwallex-webhook.js`): the original is marked paid as today; when the amount paid is more than the balance, the difference is the interest, so the webhook raises the interest invoice itself (the same code as Raise interest invoice, moved to `_shared/interest-invoice.js`), marks it paid the same moment ("Paid by card through the pay online link"), and pushes it to Xero when the original invoice is in Xero, so the Airwallex settlement matches two Xero invoices and reconciles cleanly. If the push fails the history says so and Push to Xero stays on its row. The original's history gets "Interest invoiced".
4. Bank transfer payers are unchanged: pay the balance only and the accrual stops at the payment date; Raise or Waive by hand as in item 66.
5. The invoice panel's Charge interest text adds "Pay online charges the balance plus the interest to the day they pay."
6. Offered, not assumed (owner to say): a Settings default "Charge interest on every new invoice" so the tick starts on and nobody has to remember it when an invoice goes overdue; the per-invoice tick stays to turn it off for a client who should not be charged.

Done means (staging, admin@staging.test): (1) Invoices > SI9101 > Charge interest on > Client's invoice page: Balance Due $3,300.00, "Late payment interest to 9 Nov (6 days at 21% pa, GST free)" $11.40, Total to pay today $3,311.40; the phone card shows $3,311.40; Regenerate PDF shows the same with the date; (2) Pay online now asks for $3,311.40 today and $3,313.30 tomorrow (needs an Airwallex sandbox key in Settings on staging to see the card page; without it the amount is checked on the link row in the database); (3) a paid webhook event for that link (sent by hand with the staging webhook secret): SI9101 paid, a new invoice "Interest on SI9101: 6 days to 9 Nov 2026 at 21% pa" for $11.40 marked paid at the same moment, history on both, "pushed to Xero" skipped with a reason because staging has no Xero key; (4) Charge interest off: the page shows and the link charges $3,300.00 as today; (5) a paid invoice shows no interest lines.

Touches: `public/invoice.html` (totals, phone card, button), `netlify/functions/get-or-create-payment-link.js` (amount), `netlify/functions/airwallex-webhook.js` (interest invoice on payment), `netlify/functions/raise-interest-invoice.js` and new `netlify/functions/_shared/interest-invoice.js` (the raise logic shared with the webhook), `netlify/functions/push-invoice-to-xero.js` (a callable shared entry if it is not one yet), `public/js/invoice-actions.js` (one sentence, `?v=4`), `docs/PROJECT_SPEC.md` A22 and H. Overlaps: item 67 (waiting on yes) touches `invoice.html` and `invoice-actions.js` too, so build 67 and 68 in this chat, one after the other; nothing on `staging`; open PR 34 is the agents. Kind: feature (straight to main on the owner's word, else `staging`); migration no; money yes (what the client is charged online and what reconciles in Xero), stock no, RLS no, payroll no. Size: medium (a morning). Test on staging: admin@staging.test, the five clicks above; Airwallex sandbox key on staging for the card page. Simpler: the client sees one figure, Total to pay today, and Pay online charges it; nobody works out interest by hand, raises the interest invoice or matches the card payment in Xero after an online payment. Still by hand: Raise or Waive for a bank transfer payer who pays the balance only (the bank cannot tell us what they meant), and the Charge interest tick itself (per invoice at the owner's request; point 6 above would make it start on). Best model: Fable 5.1, effort xhigh, because it changes what clients are charged and how it reconciles in Xero.

## 71. ServiceM8 deposits come across as paid claims (owner's yes 10 Nov 2026, "build, that's correct money", LIVE the same day)

**As built (10 Nov 2026):**
- J7016 data fix done on production (backup `backup_20261110.j7016_cost_centres`): the paid claim "4809 deposit" $3,123.92 ex GST ($3,436.31 inc, 28 Sep 2026, ServiceM8 Pay), `invoiced_amount` 0 to 3,123.92, and `quoted_amount` 6,620.87 to 8,092.83 (the owner's "that's correct money" to ServiceM8's current figure), so what is left to claim is $4,968.91 ex GST, $5,465.80 inc, ServiceM8's balance. The stage's 31 line items are still the ones from the move (they add up to $6,620.87): the invoice's "show quoted lines" tick should stay off on J7016 until the lines are brought up to date (Edit stages, or a line refresh from ServiceM8 in a session that can reach it; cloud sessions cannot).
- Points 2 and 3 as planned. Point 4 changed shape: cloud sessions cannot reach ServiceM8, so instead of a one-off read the review screen itself does it every time it loads: a job already in the app that ServiceM8 says was paid something has its payments read (one call), and any not on the job's stages yet show under "Deposits to add"; ticking the job and pressing Import adds them to its first stage as paid claims (matched by number, so twice adds nothing). The owner runs it once for the 30 jobs. Also fixed on the way: a ServiceM8 job whose own invoice went out with no partial claims used to lose its invoiced figure when the line items loaded (reset to $0); it now comes across with that invoice as its one claim, paid when the payments cover it.
- Proof: `npm run check` green; the deposit logic run in node against the shared module (one deposit, two deposits, a deposit beside a partial claim, an invoice paid in full and in part); the page and the function cannot be run against ServiceM8 from a cloud session, so the first live load of the import page is the check (read only until Import is pressed).

Owner (10 Nov 2026, two ServiceM8 screenshots of job #4809): J7016 is missing a deposit payment of $3,436.31 plus a $46.13 card fee.

Found (read only, production):
1. **The deposit never came across.** The import brings over ServiceM8 partial invoices (claims) only. #4809's deposit was taken as a ServiceM8 Pay payment on 28 Sep 2026 ("Deposit paid with Visa Debit") with no partial invoice, so J7016 (moved 20 Oct 2026) has `invoiced_amount` 0, no imported claim and no invoice: the claims card says "No claims raised yet" and the Invoice project panel offers 100%. The review screen did say "$3,436.31 paid" (the ServiceM8 job's `payment_amount`), but the import has nowhere to put a payment. 30 other imported jobs have no claim and no invoice either (J7010, J7014 to J7034, J7036, J7037, J7058, J7060, J7061, J7063, J7065 to J7067, J7070); any with a deposit taken the same way has the same gap.
2. **The $46.13 card fee is in the price, invisible on the line.** ServiceM8 has the fee at $0 cost and $46.13 sell. The import prices a stage from costs plus one blended markup, so a no-cost line lands at $0 and its sell goes into the markup (73.2% here): the stage's $6,620.87 ex GST already includes the $46.13. Nothing is missing from the total.
3. **ServiceM8 has moved on.** #4809 now reads $8,092.83 ex GST ($8,902.09 inc, balance $5,465.78 after the deposit); TEops still has the $6,620.87 from the move. If the job's price grew after 20 Oct, J7016 should follow (then what is left to claim matches ServiceM8's balance to the cent).

Plan (bug fix into `main`, no migration):
1. **Production data, J7016 (owner's yes first, backup `backup_20261110.j7016_cost_centres`):** one imported claim on the stage, `{ number: "4809 deposit", amount: 3123.92, date: "2026-09-28", source: "servicem8", paid: "2026-09-28", method: "ServiceM8 Pay" }` ($3,436.31 inc GST), `invoiced_amount` 0 to 3,123.92. The claims card lists it as paid, the Invoice project panel offers only what is left ($3,496.95 ex GST at today's price, or $4,968.91 if the price follows ServiceM8), the final invoice is Progress claim 2 and its Claim summary shows the deposit. No Xero push: the deposit is already in Xero through ServiceM8.
2. **Paid imported claims show as paid.** `imported_claims` entries may carry `paid` (date) and `method`; the stage's claims card prints "Paid 28/09/2026, ServiceM8 Pay" instead of "Invoiced in ServiceM8 before the move". The client's invoice already lists imported claims with their date, so `get_invoice_by_token_v3` is unchanged.
3. **The import brings deposits across by itself.** When a ServiceM8 job's payments are more than its Completed claims, the difference comes across as one paid claim "<job> deposit" with the payment's date and method (one `jobpayment` read per such job in `sm8-import-lines.js`, alongside the two calls it already makes), shown on the review screen ("deposit $3,436.31 paid 28/09/2026, comes across as a paid claim") and counted in invoiced to date; the "claims add up to the invoiced figure" check still holds.
4. **The 30 jobs already across:** the same read against ServiceM8 (the import preview is a dry run, read only in both systems) lists any with a payment no claim explains; each gets the same data fix as J7016, with the amounts in the PR for the owner's yes before the write.

Not in this build, and why: the $46.13 fee line stays at $0 on the stage. Showing it at its sell price needs a per-line sell price (`cost_centre_line_items` has only `unit_cost` and the stage markup), a schema change for no change in any total.

Done means: (1) J7016 > Cost Centres: the claims card shows "4809 deposit, $3,123.92, 28/09/2026, Paid, ServiceM8 Pay" and "No claims raised yet" is gone. (2) J7016 > Invoices > Invoice project: claimed to date shows the deposit and the panel will not claim past what is left. (3) Settings > Import from ServiceM8: #4809 reads "already imported as J7016" with "deposit $3,436.31 paid 28/09/2026" beside it, nothing written. (4) Staging J9002 > Cost Centres: the TEST stage's fake claim marked paid shows the Paid badge.

Touches: `netlify/functions/_shared/sm8-import.js` (payments per job, the deposit claim), `netlify/functions/sm8-import-lines.js`, `netlify/functions/sm8-import-jobs.js` (claims with `paid` and `method`), `public/sm8-import.html` (review screen line), `public/project.html` (claims card: Paid badge and method), `?v=` bump; production data on J7016 and any job point 4 finds. Overlaps: none (open PR 34 is the email workers; item 62 is pipeline moves; no unmerged branch touches the import or the claims card).
